# Application Security and Development Security Technical Implementation Guide: current status

Recommended status, supporting evidence and action for each requirement. Apply the stated component boundaries and obtain final checklist acceptance.

| Rule | Requirement | Status | Justification | Evidence / next action |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V-222387 / APSC-DV-000010 | The application must provide a capability to limit the number of logon sessions per user. | Needs work | UDS includes a deny-new-session limiter, but actual Core default SSO_SESSION_MAX_PER_USER=0 is unlimited. The official production check asks that the organization-defined maximum actually be configured; provider capability resolves implementation ownership but does not choose/set that maximum. The session limiter is implemented by Core; only Gallatin policy value and applied operator configuration remain. Human review on 2026-11-10 confirmed Needs work; one concurrent session per user is the proposed maximum, pending policy approval and configuration. | [re-asd-release](https://github.com/gallatinai/horizon-uds-bundle/actions/runs/42114745685) [re-asd-realm](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-identity-config/blob/74480722e1cc8405b5e9b65ac7d7086ab7f4056d/src/realm.json5) [re-asd-core-id](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-core/blob/c15677633cffa17e99656a7d7ccd1f98d7cb6e4d/src/keycloak/chart/values.yaml) [mpn-identity-values](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-core/blob/c15677633cffa17e99656a7d7ccd1f98d7cb6e4d/src/keycloak/chart/values.yaml) [mpn-identity-override](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-core/blob/c15677633cffa17e99656a7d7ccd1f98d7cb6e4d/src/keycloak/common/zarf.yaml) [mpn-identity-init-only](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-core/blob/c15677633cffa17e99656a7d7ccd1f98d7cb6e4d/docs/how-to-guides/identity-and-authorization/configure-keycloak-login-policies.mdx) [mpn-identity-realm](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-identity-config/blob/74480722e1cc8405b5e9b65ac7d7086ab7f4056d/src/realm.json5) [mpn-core-known-identity-settings](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-core/blob/c15677633cffa17e99656a7d7ccd1f98d7cb6e4d/src/keycloak/chart/values.yaml) Gallatin security confirms the proposed maximum of one concurrent session per user. Gallatin SRE sets realmInitEnv.SSO_SESSION_MAX_PER_USER to the approved positive value and updates the existing uds-user-session-count authenticator config through AdminConsole/API where needed. Verify the applied realm enforces the approved maximum. The proposed value of 1 is not yet an approved or applied configuration. No Horizon session-limiter code is needed. |

| V-222411 / APSC-DV-000320 | The application must automatically disable accounts after a 35 day period of account inactivity. | Needs work | UDS has the needed35-day inactivity workflow, but it is opt-in and ACCOUNT_INACTIVITY_DAYS is unset/default disabled in Core. This is a delegated provider setting, not missing application code. Inactivity implementation already exists in Core and is Gallatin-operated; the source-known disabled default needs Gallatin configuration, not documentation from an external provider. | V-222428 / APSC-DV-000490 | The application must enforce approved authorizations for controlling the flow of information between interconnected systems based on organization-defined information flow control policies. | Needs work | Source inspection of assessed Horizon commit b84b9af establishes an application information-flow-control function: the ingestion processor passes the file security classification to classify_document_type, which permits the document-type LLM call only for unclassified files. The client sends a content sample to the configured model service. This specific label-dependent transfer gate supports applicability; classification metadata or UDS networking alone would not establish it. | V-222520 / APSC-DV-001520 | The application must require users to reauthenticate when organization-defined circumstances or situations require reauthentication. | Needs work | The app uses live OpenFGA roles rather than a user-selected active-role mode, but a grant writes the role tuple immediately and subsequent permissionchecks use those rights under the existing signedJWT. General UDSidle/OTP does not supply this specificfresh-auth trigger. This matches the platform reauthentication gap and can be addressed through shared identity/client policy or accepted role-model tailoring rather than a duplicate authenticationimplementation. Human review on 2026-11-10 confirmed Needs work: define the reauthentication policy and enforce fresh authentication for applicable role changes and privilege escalation. Reuse shared identity with application enforcement where needed; ordinary login, idle expiry or automatic token renewal alone does not establish this trigger. | [re-asd-release](https://github.com/gallatinai/horizon-uds-bundle/actions/runs/42114745685) [re-asd-role-grants](https://github.com/gallatinai/horizon/blob/b84b9afcd6983d553b6b8406317b6592ec90cb7c/apps/api/src/api/users/user-roles.service.ts) [re-asd-live-authz](https://github.com/gallatinai/horizon/blob/b84b9afcd6983d553b6b8406317b6592ec90cb7c/apps/api/src/core/authorization/authorization.service.ts) [re-asd-auth](https://github.com/gallatinai/horizon/blob/b84b9afcd6983d553b6b8406317b6592ec90cb7c/apps/web/src/auth/auth-service.ts) [re-asd-jwt](https://github.com/gallatinai/horizon/blob/b84b9afcd6983d553b6b8406317b6592ec90cb7c/apps/api/src/api/auth/auth.oidc-jwt.service.ts) [mpn-identity-values](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-core/blob/c15677633cffa17e99656a7d7ccd1f98d7cb6e4d/src/keycloak/chart/values.yaml) [mpn-identity-override](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-core/blob/c15677633cffa17e99656a7d7ccd1f98d7cb6e4d/src/keycloak/common/zarf.yaml) Define the organization-required reauthentication circumstances, including applicable role changes and privilege escalation required by the STIG. Enforce a fresh-authentication/step-up requirement through the shared identity flow and the appropriate client/API boundary before changed or elevated rights can be exercised. Verify an existing session cannot bypass the requirement after a role grant and retain policy, implementation and test evidence. Do not treat silent token renewal alone as user reauthentication. |

| V-222620 / APSC-DV-002890 | Application web servers must be on a separate network segment from the application and database servers if it is a tiered application operating in the DoW DMZ. | Unclear | Applicability depends on whether the mission hosts this tiered application in the DoW DMZ. Existing gateway routing and broad intra-namespace allowances do not establish separation between web, application and database tiers.
